Written by `tools/augment_tiers.py` (do not edit): the test champions' tiers, 230 augments rated, 18 not. Fit is the reasoning (−2 useless … +2 the kit is what the augment rewards); games and the gap come from resg.top 16.21 (collected 2026-11-02), shown here only for review; the score is the gap pulled toward the augment's result on every champion when games are few, plus the fit. Tiers per rarity: S ≥ +3, A ≥ +1.5, B ≥ 0 points.
